as follows: (1) If
|\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}|=1
for some
k
and
j=\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}
, then take
Z^{(\ell)}_{j}=X_{j}
and
\mathfrak{K}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}=\{j\}
; (2) If
|\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}|>1
for some
k
and
\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}=\{k,j_{1},\dots j_{k}\}
, then take
\left[Z_{k}^{(\ell)},Z_{j_{1}}^{(\ell)},\dots,Z_{j_{k}}^{(\ell)}\right]=\left[X_{k},\delta_{j_{1}},\dots,\delta_{j_{k}}\right]
, and
\mathfrak{K}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}=\{k\}
; (3) If
|\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}|>1
for some
k
, and
\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}=\{j_{1},\dots j_{k}\}
where
k\not\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}
, then take
\left[Z^{(\ell)}_{j_{1}},Z^{(\ell)}_{j_{2}}\dots,Z_{j_{k}}^{(\ell)}\right]=\left[X_{j_{1}},\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{1}}^{\perp}}X_{j_{2}},\dots,\mathcal{P}_{\{X_{j_{1}},\dots,X_{j_{k-1}}\}^{\perp}}X_{j_{k}}\right]
, and
\mathfrak{K}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}=\{j_{1}\}
. We define the “quasi-proxies” as
\mathfrak{V}^{(\ell)}=\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\mathfrak{K}^{(\ell)}
.

Note that for each
k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})
, the representative features and its “quasi” version should be very close. Define
q_{k}^{(\ell)}:=\mathcal{C}_{k}\cap\mathfrak{K}^{(\ell)}
, then by Lemma
10
,
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}-\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}^{(\ell)}}}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}^{(\ell)}}})}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Step 1: find a lower bound for
\sigma_{|S|}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})
for any
S\subseteq\widetilde{S}\in\mathcal{S}
.

Let
\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}=\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\cap\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}-\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\setminus\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}}^{(\ell)}}-\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{V}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}
. We have
\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\sum_{j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\cap\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}-\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\cap\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}^{(\ell)}}}\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.
Moreover, let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}
, and
\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\|_{2}+\|\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}-\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\\
={}&\left(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\Delta\right)\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\left(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\setminus\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}^{(\ell)}}}+\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{V}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}+\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\left(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\Delta\right)\\
={}&\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\widetilde{\Delta},\end{split}

where
\|\widetilde{\Delta}\|_{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{12}\mathfrak{T}_{i}(s_{0},n,p)
. Specifically,

\mathfrak{T}_{1}=\left\|\sum_{j,k,l\in\mathrm{RF}(S)^{3}\setminus\{j=k=l\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{i}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{l}}\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{2}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\left(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\setminus\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}}^{(\ell)}}+\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{V}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\\
\leq s^{\star}s_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}D\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{3}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{4}=\left\|\sum_{j\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\left(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\setminus\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}}^{(\ell)}}+\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{V}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}s_{0}\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{5}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{6}=\left\|\sum_{j\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\right)\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{7}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\Delta\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{8}=\left\|\sum_{j\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\Delta\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\|\Delta\|_{2}^{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\log p/ns})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{9}=\left\|\sum_{j\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\Delta\left(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\setminus\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}}^{(\ell)}}+\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{V}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}s_{0}\|\Delta\|_{2}\\
\leq(s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{10}=\left\|\Delta\left(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(\widehat{S}^{(\ell)})\setminus\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{q_{k}}^{(\ell)}}+\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{V}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s_{0}\|\Delta\|_{2}^{2}\leq s_{0}(s^{\star})^{2}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{11}=\left\|\sum_{j\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\Delta\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\|\Delta\|_{2}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\right)\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{12}=\left\|\Delta\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq\|\Delta\|_{2}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\right)\leq(s^{\star})^{3}(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}})^{3/2}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}).

Combining them together, we have
\|\widetilde{\Delta}\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star}s_{0}+2(s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}+3(s^{\star})^{3})\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}+3(s^{\star})^{3})\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+(s^{\star})^{3}(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}})^{3/2}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

As a result, by Weyl’s inequality,

\sigma_{|S|}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\geq\sigma_{|S|}(\sum_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})-\|\widetilde{\Delta}\|_{2}\geq 1-(s^{\star}s_{0}+2(s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}+3(s^{\star})^{3})\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}-((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}+3(s^{\star})^{3})\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}-(s^{\star})^{3}(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}})^{3/2}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})=:L_{3},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
14
and condition (iii).

Step 2: find an upper bound for the stability of newly added direction
when adding an “undesired feature” to
S\subseteq\widetilde{S}\in\mathcal{S}
. The aim is to ensure that no extra features in a selected cluster by
S
, or features in a noise cluster can be added to
S
with high probability.

Define
r_{j}:=X_{j}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{j}
. By Lemma
15
, we know that any “undesired” features
X_{j}
, there must exist
u\in\mathcal{U}
such that
r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
or
-r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
. Now for any “undesired” feature
X_{j}
and any subsample
\ell\in\{1,\dots,B\}
, consider

{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\mathcal{P}_{r_{j}})=\frac{r_{j}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}r_{j}}{r_{j}^{\top}r_{j}}=\frac{(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)}{(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)^{\top}(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)}.

Define
\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}:=\bigcup_{k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}q_{k}^{(\ell)}
. Let
\nabla=\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}
, then
\|\nabla\|_{F}\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\|_{F}+\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\sum_{j\in\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\|_{F}+\|\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}-\sum_{k\in\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\|_{F}\leq 2s^{\star}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}^{2}+2)}}{\eta_{1}^{2}+1}+\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. For the numerator,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)=u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u+u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u\\
={}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\nabla)\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u+u^{\top}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\nabla)\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\nabla)u\\
={}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}u+2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}u+u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\nabla u\\
={}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u-u^{\top}\nabla u+2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\mathfrak{K}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}u+u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\nabla u\\
\leq{}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u+5\|\nabla\|_{F}\|u\|_{2}^{2}+\|\nabla\|^{2}_{F}\|u\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\mathcal{P}_{r_{j}})&\leq\frac{{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})}{1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})}+\frac{5\|\nabla\|_{F}+\|\nabla\|_{F}^{2}}{1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})}\\
&\leq{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})+10\|\nabla\|_{F}+2\|\nabla\|_{F}^{2}\\
&\leq{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})+20s^{\star}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}^{2}+2)}}{\eta_{1}^{2}+1}+8(s^{\star})^{2}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}^{2}+2)}{(1+\eta_{1}^{2})^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}),\end{split}

where the second inequality follows from the fact that
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\leq 1
and
\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\frac{1}{2}
by Lemma
15
and condition (ii).

Finally, for the given
\alpha_{0}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\geq 1-\alpha_{0}\right]\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{1}{B/2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B/2}\prod_{i\in\{0,1\}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(2\ell-i)}}})\geq 1-2\alpha_{0}\right]\\
\leq{}&\frac{1}{1-2\alpha_{0}}\frac{1}{B/2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B/2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(2\ell)}}})\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(2\ell-1)}}})\right]\leq\frac{\gamma_{\mathcal{U}}^{2}}{1-2\alpha_{0}},\end{split}

and hence
\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\geq 1-\alpha_{0}\right]\leq|\mathcal{U}|\gamma_{\mathcal{U}}^{2}/(1-2\alpha_{0})=\left(s^{\star}\binom{D-1}{2}+p-s^{\star}\right)\gamma_{\mathcal{U}}^{2}/(1-2\alpha_{0})
.

In summary, with probability at least
1-\left(s^{\star}\binom{D-1}{2}+p-s^{\star}\right)\gamma_{\mathcal{U}}^{2}/(1-2\alpha_{0})
, for any “undesired” feature
X_{j}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{r_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg})&\leq\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})+20s^{\star}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}^{2}+2)}}{\eta_{1}^{2}+1}+8(s^{\star})^{2}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}^{2}+2)}{(1+\eta_{1}^{2})^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})\\
&\leq 1-\alpha_{0}+20s^{\star}\frac{\sqrt{2\eta_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}^{2}+2)}}{\eta_{1}^{2}+1}+8(s^{\star})^{2}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}^{2}+2)}{(1+\eta_{1}^{2})^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})=:U_{3}.\end{split}

Step 3: Draw the conclusion
.
When
U_{3}<\alpha<L_{3}
, the only selection sets can be returned by our algorithm are those in
\{S:S\subseteq\widetilde{S}\in\mathcal{S}\}
. Note that
\sigma_{|S|}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\inf_{Z\in{\rm span}(X_{S})}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{Z}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg})
, implying that for any
S\in\mathcal{S}
, both the “new direction condition” and the “
\sigma_{\min}
-condition" will be satisfied and hence only
S
rather than its subsets will be returned.

D.2.4
Proof of Theorem
3

The notation
\mathrm{RF}(S)
used in this proof is defined at the beginning of Section
D.2
.

Proof.

We begin by giving a brief outline of the proof. Note that for any given selection set
S
, the objective function of
\ell_{0}
-penalized regression can be re-written as its prediction error
\mathrm{pe}(S)
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}y\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}}\beta_{k}^{\star}\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}+\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&=\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\left|\beta^{\star}_{k}\right|^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sum_{k\neq l\in S^{\star}}\beta_{k}^{\star}\beta_{l}^{\star}X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}+2\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\beta^{\star}_{k}\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}.\end{split}

Notice that
\mathrm{pe}(S)
decreases as
S
expands, so the solution should always select the maximum number of features, i.e.
s_{0}
features. Accordingly, we develop the following proofs by comparing the objective function values for different selection sets
S
with
|S|=s_{0}
, and find the selection set that minimizes the objective function.

Part (1).
Denote by
S^{\star}_{+}:=\mathrm{RF}(S)\cap S^{\star}
the signal representative features that are captured by
S
, and denote by
S^{\star}_{-}:=S^{\star}\setminus S^{\star}_{+}
the signal representative features that are missed by
S
.

Note that multiple features within one cluster may be selected by
S
. We create a set of new basis vectors
\{Z_{1},\dots,Z_{|S|}\}
for
{\rm span}(X_{S})
as follows: (1) If
|S\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}|=1
for some
k
and
j=S\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}
, then take
Z_{j}=X_{j}
; (2) If
|S\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}|>1
for some
k
and
S\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}=\{k,j_{1},\dots j_{k}\}
, then take
\left[Z_{k},Z_{j_{1}},\dots,Z_{j_{k}}\right]=\left[X_{k},\delta_{j_{1}},\dots,\delta_{j_{k}}\right]
; (3) If
|S\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}|>1
for some
k
, and
S\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}=\{j_{1},\dots j_{k}\}
where
k\not\in S\cap\mathcal{C}_{k}
, then take
\left[Z_{j_{1}},Z_{j_{2}}\dots,Z_{j_{k}}\right]=\left[X_{j_{1}},\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{1}}^{\perp}}X_{j_{2}},\dots,\mathcal{P}_{\{X_{j_{1}},\dots,X_{j_{k-1}}\}^{\perp}}X_{j_{k}}\right]
. Now let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}
. By Lemma
11
and
12
, we have
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\left(2\sum_{j<l\in S}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{l}})\right)^{1/2}\leq|S|\max_{j<l\in S}\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{l}}\|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Step 1: consider the scenario when
S
misses no signal groups
, which is
S^{\star}=S^{\star}_{+}
.

For any
k\in S^{\star}
, there must be one
j_{0}\in S
such that
\mathrm{RF}(j_{0})=k
and
Z_{j_{0}}=X_{j_{0}}
. Hence,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{j_{0}\}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}
. By Lemma
10
, we know
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})\leq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. By Lemma
12
,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}={\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})\leq\mathcal{O}(D^{2}\log p/n)
for any
j\in S\setminus\mathcal{C}_{k}
.
For any
j\in\mathcal{C}_{k}\setminus\{j_{0}\}
,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}})\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}
. Furthermore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}).

As a result,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{D\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\neq l\in S^{\star}
, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq\left|X_{k}^{\top}X_{l}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{l}\right|+\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}\right|
. Note that for any
j\in S
, either
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{k}
or
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{l}
; without loss of generality, we assume that
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{l}
. By Lemma
12
, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{l}\right|\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{l}\right\|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}(D\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Furthermore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}\right|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\in S^{\star}
, we have
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\left|\epsilon^{\top}X_{k}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{k}\right|+\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|
. By Lemma
12
, for any
j\in S
, we have
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}(D\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Furthermore,
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Hence,
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Finally, note that
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}
. In summary, when
S\in\mathcal{S}
,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\leq U_{1}:=\frac{D\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.

Step 2: consider the scenario when
S
misses
r
signal groups
, which is
|S^{\star}_{-}|=r\geq 1
.

Now for any
k\in S^{\star}_{+}
,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq 0
. For any
k\in S^{\star}_{-}
, on the other hand,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}=1-\sum_{j\in S}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}.

By Lemma
12
, we have

\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}={\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})\leq\mathcal{O}\left(D^{2}\log p/n\right)
for any
j\in S
. Furthermore,

\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}).

As a result,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq 1-\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
for
k\in S^{\star}_{-}
.

Similarly as in step 1, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
, and
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
for any
k\neq l\in S^{\star}
.

Finally,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}=\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{j\in S}\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon
. By Lemma
12
, for any
j\in S
, we have
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\epsilon\right\|^{2}_{2}=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{\epsilon})\leq\|\epsilon\|^{2}_{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}(D^{2}\log p/n)
, and
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon\right|_{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\left[1-\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})\right]
.

In summary, when
|S^{\star}_{-}|=r\geq 1
,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\geq L_{1}(r):=r\min_{j\in S^{\star}}\left|\beta_{j}^{\star}\right|^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.

Step 3: Find a sufficient condition for not missing any signal groups
. Note that when

\min_{j\in S^{\star}}\left|\beta^{\star}_{j}\right|^{2}>\frac{D\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}(s_{0}D^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}
, we have
L_{1}(r)>U_{1}
for any
r\geq 1
, meaning that no signal groups should be missed.

Part (2).

When
s_{0}=s^{\star}
, the only solutions that miss no signal groups are those selecting exactly one feature per signal group. In other words,
{\rm supp}(\widehat{\beta})\in\mathcal{S}
.

Part (3).

Step 1: consider the scenario when
S\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{S^{\star}\}
. Let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}
, and by Lemma
10
and
11
, we have
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\in S^{\star}
, there must be one and only one
j_{0}\in S
such that
\mathrm{RF}(j_{0})=k
. Hence,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{j_{0}\}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}
. By Lemma
13
, we know
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. By Lemma
10
,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}={\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})\leq\mathcal{O}(\log p/n)
for any
j\in S\setminus\{j_{0}\}
. Furthermore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n}).

As a result,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}-s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\neq l\in S^{\star}
, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq\left|X_{k}^{\top}X_{l}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}\right|+\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}\right|
. Note that for any
j\in S
, either
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{k}
or
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{l}
; without loss of generality, we assume that
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{l}
. By Lemma
10
, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}\right|\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}\right\|_{2}={\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{l}})^{1/2}\leq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Furthermore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}\right|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\in S^{\star}
, we have
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\left|\epsilon^{\top}X_{k}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right|+\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|
. By Lemma
10
, for any
j\in S
, we have
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Furthermore,
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Hence,
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Finally,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}=\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{j\in S}\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon
. By Lemma
10
, for any
j\in S
, we have
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\epsilon\right\|^{2}_{2}=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{\epsilon})\leq\|\epsilon\|^{2}_{2}\mathcal{O}(\log p/n)
, and
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon\right|_{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\left[1-\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})\right]
.

In summary, when
S\in\mathcal{S}\setminus\{S^{\star}\}
,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\geq L_{2}:=\min_{j\in S^{\star}}\left|\beta_{j}^{\star}\right|^{2}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.

Step 2: Find a sufficient condition for preferring
S^{\star}
over
\mathcal{S}\setminus\{S^{\star}\}
.
Note that
\mathrm{pe}(S^{\star})=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{(X_{S^{\star}})^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}
. When

\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}>\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2},

we have
L_{2}>\mathrm{pe}(S^{\star})
, meaning that
S^{\star}
is more preferred by
\widehat{\beta}
.

D.2.5
Proof of Proposition
4
and Corollary
5

Proof.

Let
\nabla=\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}\left((\mathcal{P}_{X}-\sum_{j\in[p]}\mathcal{P}_{w_{j}})\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\right)\right]
.
By Assumption
1
, we have

\frac{\sum_{j\in W}\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{w_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\right]}{p-s^{\star}}\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}|\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}|-\sum_{j\in W}\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{w_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\right]-\nabla}{s^{\star}},

which, combined with Assumption
2
, implies that

(p-s^{\star})l\leq\sum_{j\in W}\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{w_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\right]\leq\frac{p-s^{\star}}{p}(\mathbb{E}|\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}|-\nabla),

and hence
l\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}|\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}|-\nabla}{p}\leq\frac{s_{0}-\nabla}{p}
, where
|\nabla|\leq s_{0}\|\mathcal{P}_{X}-\sum_{j\in[p]}\mathcal{P}_{w_{j}}\|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}p\sqrt{\log p/n})
. As a result,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{j\in W}\sup_{T\in\mathscr{T}}\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{w_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}(T)}})\right]\leq\sum_{j\in W}(l+r_{j})=\sum_{j\in W}\left[\frac{s_{0}-\nabla}{p}+r_{j}\right]\leq s_{0}+\sum_{j\in[p]}r_{j}+\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}p\sqrt{\log p/n}\right),\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{j\in W}\sup_{T\in\mathscr{T}}\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{w_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}(T)}})\right]^{2}\leq\sum_{j\in W}(l+r_{j})^{2}=\sum_{j\in W}\left[\frac{s_{0}-\nabla}{p}+r_{j}\right]^{2}\\
\leq{}&\sum_{j\in W}\left[\frac{s_{0}^{2}}{p^{2}}+r_{j}^{2}+\frac{2s_{0}}{p}r_{j}+\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\right]\leq\frac{s_{0}^{2}}{p}+\sum_{j\in[p]}\left[r_{j}^{2}+\frac{2s_{0}}{p}{r_{j}}\right]+\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}^{2}p\sqrt{\log p/n}\right).\end{split}

Plugging these terms into the result of Theorem
1
, the result of Corollary
5
follows.

D.2.6
Proof of equation (
15
)

Proof.

In the special case, the base procedure
\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
would include
S^{\star}
and uniformly select
(s_{0}-s^{\star})
other directions from
\bigcup_{k\in S^{\star}}\{\delta_{j}:j\in\mathcal{V}_{k}\}\cup\bigcup_{k\notin S^{\star}}\{X_{j}:j\in\mathcal{C}_{k}\}
.

For any
u=\delta_{j}\in\bigcup_{k\in S^{\star}}\{\delta_{j}:j\in\mathcal{V}_{k}\}
, we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=1
w.p.
\frac{s_{0}-s^{\star}}{p-s^{\star}}
, and
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=0
otherwise.

For any
u=\delta_{j}-\delta_{l}\in\bigcup_{k\in S^{\star}}\{\delta_{j}-\delta_{l}:j\neq l\in\mathcal{V}_{k}\}
, if both
j,k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
(w.p.
\frac{\binom{2}{2}\binom{p-s^{\star}-2}{s_{0}-s^{\star}-2}}{\binom{p-s^{\star}}{s_{0}-s^{\star}}}=\frac{(s_{0}-s^{\star})(s_{0}-s^{\star}-1)}{(p-s^{\star})(p-s^{\star}-1)}
), we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=1
. If either
j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
or
l\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
(w.p.
\frac{\binom{2}{1}\binom{p-s^{\star}-2}{s_{0}-s^{\star}-1}}{\binom{p-s^{\star}}{s_{0}-s^{\star}}}=\frac{2(s_{0}-s^{\star})(p-s_{0})}{(p-s^{\star})(p-s^{\star}-1)}
), we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{2(1+\eta_{1}^{2})}
. If
j,k\notin\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=0
otherwise.

For any
u=X_{j}\in\bigcup_{k\in\mathcal{K}\cap S^{\star}}\{X_{j}:j\in\mathcal{C}_{k}\}
, if
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{RF}(j)}\cap\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\neq\varnothing
(w.p.
1-\frac{\binom{D}{0}\binom{p-s^{\star}-D}{s_{0}-s^{\star}}}{\binom{p-s^{\star}}{s_{0}-s^{\star}}}=1-\frac{(p-s_{0})(p-s_{0}-1)\cdots(p-s_{0}-D+1)}{(p-s^{\star})(p-s^{\star}-1)\cdots(p-s^{\star}-D+1)}
), we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\leq 1
. Otherwise,
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}})=0
.

D.2.7
Proof of equation (
16
)

Proof.

Note that when
\epsilon\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2}/nI_{n})
, we have
\max_{j\in\mathcal{K}}\mathrm{Corr}(X_{j},\epsilon)\leq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
,
\max_{j\in\mathcal{V}}\mathrm{Corr}(\delta_{j},\epsilon)\leq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
, and
\|\epsilon\|_{2}\leq\sigma+\mathcal{O}(\sigma/\sqrt{n})
with high probability. These results follow from the concentration inequalities: for any
t>0
,
\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{j\in\mathcal{K}}\mathrm{Corr}(X_{j},\epsilon)\leq t,\max_{j\in\mathcal{V}}\mathrm{Corr}(\delta_{j},\epsilon)\leq t\right]\geq 1-ap\exp\{-bnt^{2}\}
and
\mathbb{P}[\left|\|\epsilon\|_{2}-\sigma\right|\leq t]\geq 1-2\exp(-ct^{2})
with some
a,b,c>0
.

Now since
\mathfrak{S}\subseteq\mathcal{S}
, the goal amounts to finding an upper bound and lower bound of
\mathrm{pe}(S)
for all
S\in\mathcal{S}
. Let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}
, and by Lemma
10
and
11
, we have
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Step 1: find a lower bound for
\mathrm{pe}(S)
for all
S\in\mathcal{S}
. For any
k\in S^{\star}
, we have
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\|_{2}^{2}\geq 0
.

For any
k\neq l\in S^{\star}
, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq\left|X_{k}^{\top}X_{l}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}\right|+\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}\right|
. Note that for any
j\in S
, either
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{k}
or
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{l}
; without loss of generality, we assume that
j\notin\mathcal{C}_{l}
. By Lemma
10
, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}\right|\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}\right\|_{2}={\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{l}})^{1/2}\leq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Furthermore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}\right|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\in S^{\star}
, we have
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\left|\epsilon^{\top}X_{k}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right|+\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|
. By Lemma
10
, for any
j\in S
, we have
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Furthermore,
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Hence,
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Finally,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}=\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{j\in S}\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon
. By Lemma
10
, for any
j\in S
, we have
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\epsilon\right\|^{2}_{2}=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{\epsilon})\leq\|\epsilon\|^{2}_{2}\mathcal{O}(\log p/n)
, and
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon\right|_{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\left[1-\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})\right]
.

In summary, when
S\in\mathcal{S}
,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\geq L_{3}:=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\epsilon\|_{2}+\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}\right)^{2}.

Step 2: find an upper bound for
\mathrm{pe}(S)
for all
S\in\mathcal{S}
.

For any
k\in S^{\star}
, there must be one and only one
j_{0}\in S
such that
\mathrm{RF}(j_{0})=k
. Hence,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{j_{0}\}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}
. By Lemma
10
, we know
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Furthermore,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}={\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})\leq\mathcal{O}(\log p/n)
for any
j\in S\setminus\{j_{0}\}
. Furthermore,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n}).

As a result,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Similarly as the step 1, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
, and
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
for any
k\neq l\in S^{\star}
. Finally,
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}
.

In summary, when
S\in\mathcal{S}
,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\leq U_{3}:=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.

Step 3: Draw the conclusion
. The prediction error is upper bounded by
U_{3}-L_{3}
:

\max_{S_{1}\neq S_{2}\in\mathfrak{S}}\left|\mathrm{pe}(S_{1})-\mathrm{pe}(S_{2})\right|\leq U_{3}-L_{3}.

D.3
The complex dependency setup

D.3.1
Preliminaries

Lemma 16

Suppose that

\max\left\{\max_{k\neq k^{\prime}\in\mathcal{K}\cup\mathcal{I}}\left|X_{k}^{\top}X_{k^{\prime}}\right|,\ \max_{k\in\mathcal{K}\cup\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|X_{k}^{\top}\delta\right|}{\|X_{k}\|_{2}\cdot\|\delta\|_{2}}\right\}\leq\eta_{0},

and
\eta_{0}=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. We further assume that
K^{2}\eta_{0}\rightarrow 0
as
p,n\rightarrow\infty
.
Then we have

(1)

For any
j\in\mathcal{K}
, we have

{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\delta}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}+\delta})\in\left[0,\frac{(\eta_{0}+\eta_{1})^{2}}{1-2\eta_{1}\eta_{0}}\right].

Additionally,
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\delta}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}+\delta})\leq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

(2)

For any
j\in\mathcal{K}
and
k\in\mathcal{I}
, we have

{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}+\delta})\in\left[0,\frac{\eta_{0}^{2}(1+\eta_{1})^{2}}{1-2\eta_{1}\eta_{0}}\right].

Additionally,
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}+\delta})\leq\mathcal{O}(\log p/n)
.

(3)

For any
S\subseteq\mathcal{K}
,
k\in\mathcal{K}
but
k\notin S
, define
Z=\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta
.

Then
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{Z})\in\left[0,\ \frac{\eta_{0}^{2}(|S|+\eta_{1})^{2}}{|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}-|S|(|S|-1)\eta_{0}-2|S|\eta_{0}\eta_{1}}\right]
.
Additionally,
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{Z})\leq\mathcal{O}(|S|\log p/n)
.

(4)

For any
S\subseteq\mathcal{K}
, define
Z=\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta
. Then
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\epsilon}\mathcal{P}_{Z})\in\left[0,\ \frac{\eta_{0}^{2}(|S|+\eta_{1})^{2}}{|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}-|S|(|S|-1)\eta_{0}-2|S|\eta_{0}\eta_{1}}\right]
.

Additionally,
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\epsilon}\mathcal{P}_{Z})\leq\mathcal{O}(|S|\log p/n)
.

(5)

For any
S\subseteq\mathcal{K}
and
\widetilde{S}\subseteq S
, define
Z=\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta
. Then

\left(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star}X_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}\left(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star}X_{j}\right)\leq\frac{(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star})^{2}+\|\beta^{\star}_{\widetilde{S}}\|_{1}^{2}(t^{2}\eta_{0}^{2}+2t\eta_{0})}{|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}-|S|(|S|-1)\eta_{0}-2|S|\eta_{1}\eta_{0}}.

where
t=|S|+|\widetilde{S}|+\eta_{1}
.

Additionally,

\left(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star}X_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}\left(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star}X_{j}\right)\leq\frac{(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star})^{2}}{|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})\cdot\|\beta^{\star}_{\widetilde{S}}\|_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

Part (1).
Let
\xi=\delta/\|\delta\|_{2}
, then

{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\delta}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}+\delta})=\frac{|\delta^{\top}(X_{j}+\delta)|^{2}}{\|\delta\|^{2}_{2}\cdot\|X_{j}+\delta\|^{2}_{2}}\leq\frac{(|\xi^{\top}X_{j}|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2}+\eta_{1}^{2})^{2}}{(1-2|X^{\top}_{j}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2})\cdot\eta_{1}^{2}}\leq\frac{(\eta_{0}+\eta_{1})^{2}}{1-2\eta_{1}\eta_{0}}.

Part (2).
Let
\xi=\delta/\|\delta\|_{2}
, then

{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}+\delta})=\frac{|X_{k}^{\top}(X_{j}+\delta)|^{2}}{\|X_{k}\|^{2}_{2}\cdot\|X_{j}+\delta\|^{2}_{2}}=\frac{(|X_{k}^{\top}X_{j}|+|X_{k}^{\top}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2})^{2}}{1-2|X^{\top}_{j}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2}}\leq\frac{(1+\eta_{1})^{2}\eta_{0}^{2}}{1-2\eta_{1}\eta_{0}}.

Part (3).
Let
\xi=\delta/\|\delta\|_{2}
, then

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{Z})&=\frac{|X_{k}^{\top}(\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta)|^{2}}{\|X_{k}\|^{2}_{2}\cdot\|\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta\|^{2}_{2}}\leq\frac{(\sum_{j\in S}|X_{k}^{\top}X_{j}|+|X_{k}^{\top}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2})^{2}}{|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}-2\sum_{j<k\in S}|X^{\top}_{j}X_{k}|-2\sum_{j\in S}|X_{j}^{\top}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{\eta_{0}^{2}(|S|+\eta_{1})^{2}}{|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}-|S|(|S|-1)\eta_{0}-2|S|\eta_{0}\eta_{1}}.\end{split}

Part (4).
Let
\xi=\delta/\|\delta\|_{2}
, and let
\varepsilon=\epsilon/\|\epsilon\|_{2}
. Then

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\epsilon}\mathcal{P}_{Z})&=\frac{|\epsilon^{\top}(\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta)|^{2}}{\|\epsilon\|^{2}_{2}\cdot\|\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta\|^{2}_{2}}\leq\frac{(\sum_{j\in S}|\varepsilon^{\top}X_{j}|\cdot\|\epsilon\|_{2}+|\varepsilon^{\top}\xi|\cdot\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\|\delta\|_{2})^{2}}{\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot(|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}-2\sum_{j<k\in S}|X^{\top}_{j}X_{k}|-2\sum_{j\in S}|X_{j}^{\top}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2})}\\
&\leq\frac{\eta_{0}^{2}(|S|+\eta_{1})^{2}}{|S|+\eta_{1}^{2}-|S|(|S|-1)\eta_{0}-2|S|\eta_{0}\eta_{1}}.\end{split}

Part (5).
Let
\xi=\delta/\|\delta\|_{2}
. Then

\left(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star}X_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{Z}}\left(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta_{j}^{\star}X_{j}\right)=\frac{\left[(\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta)^{\top}(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j})\right]^{2}}{\|\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta\|^{2}_{2}}
.
The numerator is upper bounded by
\left[(\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta)^{\top}(\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j})\right]^{2}\\
=\left[\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta^{\star}_{j}+\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}^{\top}(2\sum_{k\in\widetilde{S},k\neq j}X_{k}+\sum_{k\in S\setminus\widetilde{S}}X_{k}+\delta)\right]^{2}\\
\leq\left[\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta^{\star}_{j}\right]^{2}+\left[\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}|\left(2\sum_{k\in\widetilde{S},k\neq j}|X_{j}^{\top}X_{k}|+\sum_{k\in S\setminus\widetilde{S}}|X_{j}^{\top}X_{k}|+|X_{j}^{\top}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2}\right)\right]^{2}\\
+2\left[\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|\right]\left[\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}|\left(2\sum_{k\in\widetilde{S},k\neq j}|X_{j}^{\top}X_{k}|+\sum_{k\in S\setminus\widetilde{S}}|X_{j}^{\top}X_{k}|+|X_{j}^{\top}\xi|\cdot\|\delta\|_{2}\right)\right]\\
\leq\left[\sum_{j\in\widetilde{S}}\beta^{\star}_{j}\right]^{2}+\|\beta^{\star}_{\widetilde{S}}\|_{1}^{2}(t^{2}\eta_{0}^{2}+2t\eta_{0}),\\

where
t=|S|+|\widetilde{S}|+\eta_{1}
. The denominator is given by

\|\sum_{j\in S}X_{j}+\delta\|_{2}^{2}\geq 1+\eta_{1}^{2}-|S|(|S|-1)\eta_{0}-2|S|\eta_{0}\eta_{1}
.

Lemma 17

Given any
S\in\mathcal{S}
and the set of “undesired” directions
\mathcal{U}
in Theorem
7
:

(1)

Suppose that
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing
. For any features
X_{j}
where
j\in\mathcal{B}\setminus S\cup N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
, denote
r_{j}:=X_{j}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{j}
. Then there must exist
u\in\mathcal{U}
such that
r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
or
-r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
.

(2)

Suppose that
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\neq\varnothing
. For any features
X_{j}
where
j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cup\{c\}\cup N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
, denote
r_{j}:=X_{j}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{j}
. Then there must exist
u\in\mathcal{U}
such that
r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
or
-r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
.

(3)

The
\mathcal{U}
set is far away from
{\rm span}(X_{S})
:
\max_{S\in\mathcal{S},\ u\in\mathcal{U}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\max\left\{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \frac{|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|}{K+\eta_{1}^{2}}\right\}+\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}(K+s^{\star})\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Proof.

Part (1).

Consider the following cases: (1) If
j\in\mathcal{B}
and
c\notin S
, then we must have
S=\mathcal{K}
and
j=c
. Thus
r_{j}=X_{c}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{c}=(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}}X_{k}+\delta)-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}}X_{k}+\delta)=\delta-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\delta
, meaning that
u=\delta\in\mathcal{U}
. (2) If
j\in\mathcal{B}
and
c\in S
, then we must have
j\in\mathcal{K}
and
X_{j}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{S})
. Thus
r_{j}=X_{j}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{j}=(X_{j}+\delta-\delta)-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}(X_{j}+\delta-\delta)=-\delta+\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\delta
, meaning that
u=\delta\in\mathcal{U}
. (3) If
j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
, then it is trivial that
u=X_{j}\in\mathcal{U}
.

Part (2).

It is trivial that
u=X_{j}\in\mathcal{U}
for all
j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cup\{C\}\cup N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
.

Part (3).
Consider the following cases: (1) The scenario when
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing
. If
u=\delta
, we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\delta}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\max_{j_{0}\in\mathcal{K}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{\delta}(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\setminus\{j_{0}\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}+\delta}+\Delta))\leq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
, where
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{\{X_{j}\}_{j\in S^{\star}\setminus\{j_{0}\}}\cup\{X_{j_{0}+\delta}\}}-\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\setminus\{j_{0}\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}+\delta}
, and
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. On the other hand, if
u=X_{j}
with
j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
, then we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\max_{j_{0}\in\mathcal{K}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\setminus\{j_{0}\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}+\delta}+\Delta))\leq s^{\star}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

(2) The scenario when
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\neq\varnothing
. If
u=X_{j}
where
j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cup N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
, we then have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})={\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}X_{k}+\Delta))\leq\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
, where
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}X_{k}
with
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. On the other hand, if
u=X_{c}
, then we have

{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})=\frac{(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}}X_{k}+\delta)^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}}X_{k}+\delta)}{\|\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}}X_{k}+\delta\|_{2}^{2}}=\frac{|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|}{K+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(K^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Therefore,

\max_{S\in\mathcal{S},\ u\in\mathcal{U}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\max\left\{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \frac{|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|}{K+\eta_{1}^{2}}\right\}+\mathcal{O}(K(K+s^{\star})\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

D.3.2
Proof of Theorem
6

Proof.

The prediction error for
S
is given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&=\left\|\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}y\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}\cup\mathcal{I}}\beta_{k}^{\star}\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}X_{k}+\mathcal{P}_{{X_{S}}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&=\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\left|\beta^{\star}_{k}\right|^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sum_{k\neq l\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}\beta_{l}^{\star}X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}+2\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}.\end{split}

Part (1a): the scenario when
c\not\in S
.
Let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}
. By Lemma
11
, we have
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Step 1: consider the scenario when
S
misses
r
signal features
. In other words,
|S^{\star}\cap S^{c}|=r
. For any
k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}
,
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\|_{2}^{2}=X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}=1-\sum_{j\in S}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})-X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}
, where
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})\leq\mathcal{O}(\log p/n)
, and
|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{k}|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. As a result,
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\|_{2}^{2}\geq 1-\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\neq l\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}
, we have
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|\leq\left|X_{k}^{\top}X_{l}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}\right|+\left|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}\right|
, where
|X_{k}^{\top}X_{l}|\leq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
,
|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{l}|\leq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
, and
|X_{k}^{\top}\Delta X_{l}|\leq\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. As a result,
\left|X_{k}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{l}\right|\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For any
k\in S^{\star}
, we have
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\right|\leq\left|\epsilon^{\top}X_{k}\right|+\sum_{j\in S}\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}\right|+\left|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}\right|
, where
|\epsilon^{\top}X_{k}|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
,
|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}X_{k}|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
, and
|\epsilon^{\top}\Delta X_{k}|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. As a result,
|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Finally,
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}=\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\sum_{j\in S}\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon
, where
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\mathcal{O}(\log p/n)
, and
\epsilon^{\top}\Delta\epsilon\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. As a result,
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\left[1-\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})\right]
.

In summary, when
|S^{\star}\cap S^{c}|=r
,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\geq L_{1}(r):=r\min_{j\in S^{\star}}\left|\beta_{j}^{\star}\right|^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.

Step 2: consider the scenario when
S
misses no signals, and draw the conclusion
. Note that when
c\notin S
, missing no signals implies
S=S^{\star}
. Note that
\mathrm{pe}(S^{\star})=\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}
. Therefore, under the beta-min condition
17
, we have
\mathrm{pe}(S^{\star})\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}<\min_{r\geq 1}L_{1}(r)\leq\mathrm{pe}(S)
for any
S
such that
|S^{\star}\cap S^{c}|=r\geq 1
. Hence
S^{\star}
is more preferred by
\widehat{\beta}
.

Part (1b): the scenario when
c\in S
and
N_{\mathcal{B}}^{\star}=\varnothing
.

Step 1: consider the scenario when
S
misses signal features in
\mathcal{B}
. Note that when
|S_{\mathcal{B}}|=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|
, all block signals are regarded as being “selected”, since their subspaces are similar up to
\delta
. Suppose that
r_{1}:=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}|\geq 2
signals in
\mathcal{B}
and
r_{2}:=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}|\geq 0
signals in
\mathcal{I}
are missed. Then the prediction error is given by:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&=\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}+\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt+2\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)+2\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}.\end{split}

(18)

Let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}X_{j}+\delta}
, where
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq(s_{0}+K)\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For the first term in (
18
),
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}
. Specifically,

\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}-2\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}||X_{j}X_{k}|\\
\geq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}-\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}|\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n}).

Moreover,

\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right)^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right)\\
=\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}X_{j}+\delta}+\Delta\right)\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right)\\
\leq\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})+\frac{\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{j}^{\star}\right)^{2}}{r_{1}+\eta_{1}^{2}}.

As a result,
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
\geq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}-\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}|\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})-\frac{\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{j}^{\star}\right)^{2}}{r_{1}+\eta_{1}^{2}}-\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})\\
\geq\frac{1}{r_{1}+\eta_{1}^{2}}\left(\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}+\eta_{1}^{2}\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}\right)-\left(\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}|+\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\right)\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}).

For the second term in (
18
),
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}
.
Specifically,
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}-\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}|\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Moreover,
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
=\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}X_{j}+\delta}+\Delta\right)\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right)\\
\leq\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

As a result,
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
\geq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}-\left(\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}|+\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\right)\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For the third term in (
18
),
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}=\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}
.

Specifically,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}=\epsilon^{\top}\left(\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}X_{j}+\delta}+\Delta\right)\epsilon\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

As a result,
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For the fourth term in (
18
),
\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\\
=\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)-\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)
.

Specifically,
\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\leq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{k}\beta^{\star}_{j}|\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n}).

Moreover,
\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\\
=\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\left(\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}X_{j}+\delta}+\Delta\right)\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\\
\leq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{k}\beta^{\star}_{j}|\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

As a result,
\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\leq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{k}\beta^{\star}_{j}|\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For the fifth term in (
18
),
\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}\\
=\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta_{k}^{\star}\epsilon^{\top}X_{k}-\epsilon^{\top}\left(\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}X_{j}+\delta}+\Delta\right)\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}\right)\\
\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&\geq L_{2}(r_{1},r_{2}):=\frac{\binom{r_{1}}{2}\min_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}+r_{1}\eta_{1}^{2}\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}}{r_{1}+\eta_{1}^{2}}+r_{2}\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.\end{split}

Step 2: consider the scenario when
S
misses signals in
\mathcal{I}
but not
\mathcal{B}
, which means that
|S_{\mathcal{B}}|=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|
, but
r_{2}=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}|\geq 1
. In this case, there exists at most one element in
S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}
and let
\{j_{0}\}=S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}
. Then the prediction error is given by:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&=\left|\beta^{\star}_{j_{0}}\right|^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{j_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}+\beta^{\star}_{j_{0}}X_{j_{0}}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt+2\sum_{k\in\{j_{0}\}\cup(S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c})}\beta^{\star}_{k}\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}.\end{split}

Let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}
where
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.
Note that
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{j_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq 0
. Similarly as in Step 1, we have

\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}-\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
, and

\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.
Moreover, we have

\beta^{\star}_{j_{0}}X_{j_{0}}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\leq\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{j_{0}}||\beta^{\star}_{j}|\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
, and

\sum_{k\in\{j_{0}\}\cup(S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c})}\beta^{\star}_{k}\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}).

Therefore,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\geq\widetilde{L}_{2}(r_{2}):=r_{2}\min_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2})^{2}.

Step 3: consider the scenario when
S
misses no signals
, which means that
|S_{\mathcal{B}}|=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|
and
S_{\mathcal{I}}=S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
. In this case, there exists at most one element in
S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}
and let
\{j_{0}\}=S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}
. Then the prediction error is given by:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&=\left|\beta^{\star}_{j_{0}}\right|^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{j_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\beta^{\star}_{j_{0}}\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{j_{0}}.\end{split}

Note that
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{j_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}=1-\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{j_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq 1-\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}+\delta}X_{j_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
. In addition,
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}
. Moreover,
\left|\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{j_{0}}\right|\leq\left|\epsilon^{\top}X_{j_{0}}\right|+\left|\epsilon^{\top}(\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\Delta)X_{j_{0}}\right|\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
, where
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}
with
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq\mathcal{O}(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Therefore,

\mathrm{pe}(S)\leq U_{2}:=\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|^{2}_{2}+\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right).

Step 4: draw the conclusion
. When

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\min\left\{\frac{\min_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}+2\eta_{1}^{2}\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}}{2+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}|^{2}\right\}\\
>{}&\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2})^{2},\end{split}

we have
U_{2}<L_{2}(r_{1},r_{2})
and
U_{2}<\widetilde{L}_{2}(\widetilde{r}_{2})
for any
r_{1}\geq 2
,
r_{2}\geq 0
and
\widetilde{r}_{2}\geq 1
, meaning that no signal features should be missed.

Part (1c): the scenario when
c\in S
and
N_{\mathcal{B}}^{\star}\neq\varnothing
.

Step 1: consider the scenario when
S
misses signal features in
\mathcal{B}
. Note that when
|S_{\mathcal{B}}|=K
, all block signals are regarded as being “selected”. Suppose that
r_{1}:=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}|\geq 1
signals in
\mathcal{B}
and
r_{2}:=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}|\geq 0
signals in
\mathcal{I}
are missed. Moreover, denote
r_{3}:=|N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}|\geq 0
. Additionally note that when
r_{3}=0
, we must have
r_{1}\geq 2
. Then the prediction error is the same as (
18
):

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&=\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}X_{k}+\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)+\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}.\end{split}

Let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{\sum_{j\in\mathcal{B}\cap S^{c}}X_{j}+\delta}
, where
\|\Delta\|_{F}\leq(s_{0}+K)\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Similarly as the Step 1 in Part (1b), for the first term in (
18
),

\\
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{r_{1}+r_{3}+\eta_{1}^{2}}\left(\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}+(\eta_{1}^{2}+r_{3})\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
-\left(\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}|+\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\right)\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}).

For the second term in (
18
),

\\
\left\|\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\beta_{k}^{\star}X_{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}-\left(\sum_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}\beta_{k}^{\star}|+\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}^{2}\right)\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For the third term in (
18
),
\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For the fourth term in (
18
),

\\
\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}^{\top}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\left(\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{j}X_{j}\right)\leq\sum_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cap S^{c}}\sum_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}}|\beta^{\star}_{k}\beta^{\star}_{j}|\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

For the fifth term in (
18
),

\epsilon^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\sum_{k\in S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\beta^{\star}_{k}X_{k}\leq\|\epsilon\|_{2}\cdot\|\beta^{\star}_{S^{\star}\cap S^{c}}\|_{1}\cdot\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{pe}(S)&\geq L_{3}(r_{1},r_{2}):=\frac{\binom{r_{1}}{2}\min_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}+r_{1}(\eta_{1}^{2}+r_{3})\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}}{r_{1}+r_{3}+\eta_{1}^{2}}+r_{2}\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.\end{split}

Step 2: consider the scenario when
S
misses signals in
\mathcal{I}
but
\mathcal{B}
, which means that either
S_{\mathcal{B}}=S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}
or
|S_{\mathcal{B}}|=K
, but
r_{2}=|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}\cap S^{c}|\geq 1
. Similarly as in Step 2 of Part (1b), the prediction error is given by:

\mathrm{pe}(S)\geq\widetilde{L}_{3}(r_{2}):=r_{2}\min_{k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta_{k}^{\star}|^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2})^{2}.

Step 3: consider the scenario when
S
misses no signals
, which means that either
S=S^{\star}
, or
|S_{\mathcal{B}}|=K
and
S_{\mathcal{I}}=S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
. Similarly as in Step 3 of Part (1b), the prediction error is given by:

\mathrm{pe}(S)\leq U_{3}:=\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|^{2}_{2}+\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right).

Step 4: draw the conclusion
. When

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\min\left\{\frac{\min_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}+2\eta_{1}^{2}\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}}{K+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \frac{(\eta_{1}^{2}+1)\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}}{K+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}|^{2}\right\}\\
>{}&\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2})^{2},\end{split}

we have
U_{3}<L_{3}(r_{1},r_{2})
and
U_{3}<\widetilde{L}_{2}(\widetilde{r}_{2})
for any
r_{1}\geq 1
,
r_{2}\geq 0
and
\widetilde{r}_{2}\geq 1
, meaning that no signal features should be missed.

Part (1) Summary.

Combining all results in Part (1a)–(1c) above, the condition under which that no signals are missed is given by:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\min\left\{\frac{\min_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}+2\eta_{1}^{2}\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}}{1+K+(1-K)\mathds{1}_{\{N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing\}}+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \frac{(\eta_{1}^{2}+1)\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}\mathds{1}_{\{N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\neq\varnothing\}}}{K+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}|^{2}\right\}\\
>{}&\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2})^{2},\end{split}

and additionally

\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}>\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.

Part (2).
In the case of
s_{0}=s^{\star}
, the upper bound for
\mathrm{pe}(S)
, when
S
misses no signals and
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\neq\varnothing
, can be shaper than that in Part (1). In this case, we must have
S=S^{\star}
, and hence

\mathrm{pe}(S)=\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\perp}}\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\leq\left\|\epsilon\right\|_{2}^{2}.

As a result,
the condition under which no signals are missed is given by:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\min\left\{\frac{\min_{j\neq k\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}-\beta^{\star}_{k}|^{2}\mathds{1}_{\{N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing\}}+(1+\mathds{1}_{\{N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing\}})\eta_{1}^{2}\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}}{1+K+(1-K)\mathds{1}_{\{N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing\}}+\eta_{1}^{2}},\ \min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}|\beta_{j}^{\star}|^{2}\right\}\\
>{}&\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}\|\beta^{\star}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\mathds{1}_{\{N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing\}}+\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}+K)\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2})^{2},\end{split}

and additionally

\min_{j\in S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}|\beta^{\star}_{j}|^{2}>\mathcal{O}\left(s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\left(\|\beta^{\star}\|_{1}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}\right)^{2}.

Finally, we conclude that, when
s_{0}=s^{\star}
, the only solutions that miss no signals are those
{\rm supp}(\widehat{\beta})\in\mathcal{S}
.

D.3.3
Proof of Theorem
7

Proof.

By condition (i), we know that for all
\ell\in\{1,\dots,B\}
, we have
k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
for all
k\in S^{\star}
, except possibly for one index
k_{0}\in S^{\star}
, where
X_{k_{0}}\notin{\rm span}(X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}})
while
X_{k_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}})
.
Note that
\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}+\delta}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}+\delta})}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Now consider a set of new basis vectors
\{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}\}_{j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}
for
{\rm span}(X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}})
and construct the “quasi-child”
W^{(\ell)}
as follows:
(1) If
c\notin\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, then take
Z_{j}^{(\ell)}=X_{j}
for all
j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, and let
W^{(\ell)}=0
;
(2) If
c\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
and
S^{\star}\subseteq\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, then take
Z_{j}^{(\ell)}=X_{j}
for all
j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}
, take
Z_{c}^{(\ell)}=X_{c}-\sum_{j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\cap\mathcal{B}}X_{j}+\delta
, and let
W^{(\ell)}=Z_{c}^{(\ell)}
;
(3) If
c\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
and there exists one
k_{0}\in S^{\star}
such that
S^{\star}\setminus\{k_{0}\}\subseteq\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
while
X_{k_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}})
, then take
Z_{j}^{(\ell)}=X_{j}
for all
j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}
,
Z_{c}^{(\ell)}=X_{k_{0}}+\delta
, and let
W^{(\ell)}=X_{k_{0}}
.

Step 1: find a lower bound for
\sigma_{|S|}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})
for any
S\in\mathcal{S}
.

Let
\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}=\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\sum_{k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{k}}-\mathcal{P}_{W^{(\ell)}}
. We then have
\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\leq\left\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\sum_{j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{j}^{(\ell)}}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{Z^{(\ell)}_{c}}-\mathcal{P}_{W^{(\ell)}}\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

Moreover, let
\Delta=\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}
, and
\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\|_{2}+\|\sum_{j\in S}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}-\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\\
={}&\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\Delta\right)\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\sum_{k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}\setminus S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{k}}+\mathcal{P}_{W^{(\ell)}}\mathds{1}_{W^{(\ell)}=Z^{(\ell)}_{c}}+\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\left(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\Delta\right)\\
={}&\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}+\widetilde{\Delta},\end{split}

where
\|\widetilde{\Delta}\|_{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{12}\mathfrak{T}_{i}(s_{0},n,p)
. Specifically,

\mathfrak{T}_{1}=\left\|\sum_{j,k,l\in S^{\star}\setminus\{j=k=l\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{i}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{l}}\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{3}\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{2}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\left(\sum_{k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}\setminus S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{k}}+\mathcal{P}_{W^{(\ell)}}\mathds{1}_{W^{(\ell)}=Z^{(\ell)}_{c}}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}(s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{3}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in\mathrm{RF}(S)}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{4}=\left\|\sum_{j\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\left(\sum_{k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}\setminus S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{k}}+\mathcal{P}_{W^{(\ell)}}\mathds{1}_{W^{(\ell)}=Z^{(\ell)}_{c}}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}s_{0}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{5}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}(s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{6}=\left\|\sum_{j\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\right)\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}(s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{7}=\left\|\sum_{j,k\in S^{\star}}\Delta\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq(s^{\star})^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{8}=\left\|\sum_{j\in S^{\star}}\Delta\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\|\Delta\|_{2}^{2}\leq s^{\star}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{3}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{9}=\left\|\sum_{j\in S^{\star}}\Delta\left(\sum_{k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}\setminus S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{k}}+\mathcal{P}_{W^{(\ell)}}\mathds{1}_{W^{(\ell)}=Z^{(\ell)}_{c}}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}s_{0}\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq s_{0}s^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{10}=\left\|\Delta\left(\sum_{k\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}\setminus\{c\}\setminus S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{Z_{k}}+\mathcal{P}_{W^{(\ell)}}\mathds{1}_{W^{(\ell)}=Z^{(\ell)}_{c}}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq s_{0}\|\Delta\|_{2}^{2}\leq s_{0}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}s_{0}\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{11}=\left\|\sum_{j\in S^{\star}}\Delta\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq s^{\star}\|\Delta\|_{2}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\right)\leq s^{\star}\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}(s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n})
;

\mathfrak{T}_{12}=\left\|\Delta\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\right)\Delta\right\|_{2}\leq\|\Delta\|_{2}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\|\Delta_{S}^{(\ell)}\|_{2}\right)\leq(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}})^{3/2}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}(s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n}).
