Combining them together, we have
\|\widetilde{\Delta}\|_{2}\leq(3(s^{\star})^{2}+2s^{\star}s_{0})\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+(3s^{\star}+s_{0})\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}})^{3/2}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}(s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n})
.

As a result, by Weyl’s inequality,

\sigma_{|S|}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\geq\sigma_{|S|}(\sum_{k\in S^{\star}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{k}})-\|\widetilde{\Delta}\|_{2}\geq 1-(3(s^{\star})^{2}+2s^{\star}s_{0})\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}-(3s^{\star}+s_{0})\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}-(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}})^{3/2}+\mathcal{O}((s^{\star})^{2}(s_{0}+B)\sqrt{\log p/n})=:L_{4},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
14
and condition (ii).

Step 2: find an upper bound for the stability of newly added direction
when adding an “undesired feature” to
S\in\mathcal{S}
. The aim is to ensure that (1) when
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing
, no extra features in
\mathcal{B}\setminus S
and
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
can be added to
S
with high probability; (2) when
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\neq\varnothing
, no extra features in
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\cup\{c\}
and
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
can be added to
S
with high probability.

Define
r_{j}:=X_{j}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}X_{j}
. By Lemma
17
, we know that any “undesired” features
X_{j}
, there must exist
u\in\mathcal{U}
such that
r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
or
-r_{j}=u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u
. Now for any “undesired” feature
X_{j}
and any subsample
\ell\in\{1,\dots,B\}
, consider

\displaystyle{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\mathcal{P}_{r_{j}})=\frac{r_{j}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}r_{j}}{r_{j}^{\top}r_{j}}=\frac{(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)}{(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)^{\top}(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)}.

(19)

Define the space
\mathcal{F}^{(\ell)}_{S}
as follows: (1) if
S^{\star}\subseteq\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, then let
\mathcal{F}^{(\ell)}_{S}={\rm span}(\{X_{j}^{(\ell)}\}_{j\in S^{\star}})
; (2) If there exists one
k_{0}\in S^{\star}
such that
S^{\star}\setminus\{k_{0}\}\subseteq\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
while
X_{k_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}})
, then let
\mathcal{F}^{(\ell)}_{S}={\rm span}(\{X_{j}^{(\ell)}\}_{j\in S^{\star}\setminus\{k_{0}\}}\cup\{X_{k_{0}}+\delta\})
.

Let
\nabla=\mathcal{P}_{{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}
, then

\|\nabla\|_{F}\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}-\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}+\delta}}\mathds{1}_{\{X_{j_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{S})\}}\|_{F}\\
+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}-\sum_{j\in S^{\star}\setminus\{k_{0}\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{k_{0}+\delta}}\mathds{1}_{\{X_{k_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{S})\}}\|_{F}\\
+\|\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}+\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}+\delta}}\mathds{1}_{\{X_{j_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{S})\}}-\sum_{j\in S^{\star}\setminus\{k_{0}\}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{k_{0}+\delta}}\mathds{1}_{\{X_{k_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{S})\}}\|_{F}\\
\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}+\delta}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}}\|_{F}+\|\mathcal{P}_{X_{k_{0}+\delta}}-\mathcal{P}_{X_{k_{0}}}\|_{F}+\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n})\\
\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\mathcal{O}(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n}).

For the numerator of (
19
),

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}(u-\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u)=u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u+u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u\\
={}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}-\nabla)\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u+u^{\top}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}-\nabla)\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}-\nabla)u\\
={}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}u+2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}u+u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\nabla u\\
={}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u-u^{\top}\nabla u+2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-2u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{F}_{S}^{(\ell)}}u+u^{\top}\nabla\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\nabla u\\
\leq{}&u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}u-u^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}u+5\|\nabla\|_{F}\|u\|_{2}^{2}+\|\nabla\|^{2}_{F}\|u\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}}\mathcal{P}_{r_{j}})&\leq\frac{{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})}{1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})}+\frac{5\|\nabla\|_{F}+\|\nabla\|_{F}^{2}}{1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})}\\
&\leq{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})+10\|\nabla\|_{F}+2\|\nabla\|_{F}^{2}\\
&\leq{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})+20\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\frac{8\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right),\end{split}

where the second inequality follows from the fact that
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\leq 1
and
\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\frac{1}{2}
by Lemma
17
and condition (ii).

Finally, for the given
\alpha_{0}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\geq 1-\alpha_{0}\right]\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{1}{B/2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B/2}\prod_{i\in\{0,1\}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(2\ell-i)}}})\geq 1-2\alpha_{0}\right]\\
\leq{}&\frac{1}{1-2\alpha_{0}}\frac{1}{B/2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B/2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(2\ell))}}})\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(2\ell-1)}}})\right]\leq\frac{\gamma^{2}}{1-2\alpha_{0}},\end{split}

and hence
\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}}\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})\geq 1-\alpha_{0}\right]\leq|\mathcal{U}|\gamma^{2}/(1-2\alpha_{0})=\left(p-s^{\star}\right)\gamma^{2}/(1-2\alpha_{0})
.
In summary, with probability at least
1-\left(p-s^{\star}\right)\gamma^{2}/(1-2\alpha_{0})
, for any “undesired” feature
X_{j}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{r_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg})&\leq\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\ell=1}^{B}\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})+20\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\frac{8\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)\\
&\leq 1-\alpha_{0}+20\sqrt{\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}}+\frac{8\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(s^{\star}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right)=:U_{4}.\end{split}

Step 3: Draw the conclusion
.
When
U_{4}<\alpha<L_{4}
, the only selection sets can be returned by our algorithm are those in
\{S:S\in\mathcal{S}\}
. Note that
\sigma_{|S|}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}})\leq\inf_{Z\in{\rm span}(X_{S})}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{Z}\mathcal{P}_{\rm avg})
, implying that for any
S\in\mathcal{S}
, both the “new direction condition” and the “
\sigma_{\min}
-condition" will be satisfied and hence only
S
rather than its subsets will be returned.

D.3.4
Proof of Corollary
8

Proof.

Let
S
be any FSSS selection set. When
S\in\mathcal{S}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S^{\star}}^{\perp}})&=s^{\star}-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S^{\star}}})\\
&=s^{\star}-\sum_{j\in S\setminus\{c\}}{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}^{\star}})-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}+\delta}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S^{\star}}})\mathds{1}_{\{X_{j_{0}}+\delta\in{\rm span}(X_{S})\}}-{\rm trace}(\Delta\mathcal{P}_{X_{S^{\star}}})\\
&\leq 1-{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{j_{0}}+\delta}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S^{\star}}})+s^{\star}\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{1+\eta_{1}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left((s^{\star})^{2}\sqrt{\log p/n}\right).\end{split}

Otherwise,
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S^{\star\perp}}})\leq s_{0}
. The upper bound for
\mathbb{E}\left[{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{X_{S}}\mathcal{P}_{X_{S^{\star}}^{\perp}})\right]
hence follows.

D.3.5
Proof of Remark
7

Proof.

Scenario 1:
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\neq\varnothing
.
In the special case, the base procedure
\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
would include
S^{\star}
and uniformly select
(s_{0}-s^{\star})
other directions from
\{X_{c}\}\cup\bigcup_{j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}\{X_{j}\}\cup\bigcup_{j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}\{X_{j}\}
.

For
u=X_{c}
, if
c\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
(w.p.
\frac{s_{0}-s^{\star}}{p-s^{\star}}
), we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=1
. If
c\notin\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, then
\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
must select
m
features in
N_{\mathcal{B}}^{\star}
and
s_{0}-s^{\star}
features in
N_{\mathcal{I}}^{\star}
for
m\in\{0,\dots,|N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|\}
; this happens w.p.
\frac{\binom{|N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|}{m}\binom{1}{0}\binom{p-s^{\star}-N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}-1}{s_{0}-s^{\star}-m}}{\binom{p-s^{\star}}{s_{0}-s^{\star}}}
, and in this case
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=\frac{|S^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|+m}{K+\eta_{1}^{2}}
.

For any
u=X_{j}\in\bigcup_{j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}}\{X_{j}\}
, if
j\notin\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
and
c\notin\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=0
. If
j\notin\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
but
c\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
, then
\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
must select
m
features in
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}\setminus\{j\}
and
s_{0}-s^{\star}-m
features in
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}
for
m\in\{0,\dots,|N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|-1\}
; this happens w.p.
\frac{\binom{|N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|-1}{m}\binom{1}{0}\binom{1}{1}\binom{p-s^{\star}-N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}-1}{s_{0}-s^{\star}-m}}{\binom{p-s^{\star}}{s_{0}-s^{\star}}}
, and in this case
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=\frac{1}{|N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}|-m+\eta_{1}^{2}}
.

For any
u=X_{j}\in\bigcup_{j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}\{X_{j}\}
, if
j\in\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}
(w.p.
\frac{s_{0}-s^{\star}}{p-s^{\star}}
), we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=1
; and
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=0
otherwise.

Scenario 2:
N^{\star}_{\mathcal{B}}=\varnothing
.
In the special case, the base procedure would include
S^{\star}
and uniformly select
(s_{0}-s^{\star})
other directions from
\{\delta\}\cup\bigcup_{j\in N^{\star}_{\mathcal{I}}}\{X_{j}\}
. Therefore, for any
u\in\mathcal{U}
, we have
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=1
w.p.
\frac{s_{0}-s^{\star}}{p-s^{\star}}
, and
{\rm trace}(\mathcal{P}_{u}\mathcal{P}_{X_{\widehat{S}^{(\ell)}}})=0
otherwise.
